
HUTCN(
  (fea_conv): Conv2d(3, 96, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
  (post_unet_esa): ESA(
    (conv1): Conv2d(96, 24, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
    (conv2): Conv2d(24, 24, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(2, 2))
    (conv3): Conv2d(24, 24, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
    (conv4): Conv2d(24, 96, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
    (sigmoid): Sigmoid()
    (relu): ReLU(inplace=True)
  )
  (post_unet_conv): Conv2d(96, 96, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
  (B1): P_HTCB(
    (tesa_in): TESA(
      (esa1): ESA(
        (conv1): Conv2d(96, 24, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
        (conv2): Conv2d(24, 24, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(2, 2))
        (conv3): Conv2d(24, 24, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (conv4): Conv2d(24, 96, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (sigmoid): Sigmoid()
        (relu): ReLU(inplace=True)
      )
      (esa2): ESA(
        (conv1): Conv2d(96, 24, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
        (conv2): Conv2d(24, 24, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(2, 2))
        (conv3): Conv2d(24, 24, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (conv4): Conv2d(24, 96, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (sigmoid): Sigmoid()
        (relu): ReLU(inplace=True)
      )
      (esa3): ESA(
        (conv1): Conv2d(96, 24, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
        (conv2): Conv2d(24, 24, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(2, 2))
        (conv3): Conv2d(24, 24, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (conv4): Conv2d(24, 96, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (sigmoid): Sigmoid()
        (relu): ReLU(inplace=True)
      )
    )
    (tcn1): TCN(
      (attn): PSA(
        (blocks): ModuleList(
          (0-2): 3 x PSA_Block(
            (norm_attn): LayerNorm((96,), eps=1e-05, elementwise_affine=True)
            (norm_ffn): LayerNorm((96,), eps=1e-05, elementwise_affine=True)
            (compress): Linear(in_features=384, out_features=96, bias=False)
            (qkv): Linear(in_features=96, out_features=288, bias=True)
            (crpb): ContinuousRPB(
              (mlp): Sequential(
                (0): Linear(in_features=2, out_features=16, bias=True)
                (1): ReLU(inplace=True)
                (2): Linear(in_features=16, out_features=2, bias=False)
              )
            )
            (proj): Linear(in_features=96, out_features=96, bias=True)
            (attn_drop): Dropout(p=0.0, inplace=False)
            (proj_drop): Dropout(p=0.0, inplace=False)
            (expand): Linear(in_features=96, out_features=384, bias=False)
            (ffn): LightFFN(
              (fc1): Linear(in_features=96, out_features=96, bias=True)
              (dw): Conv2d(96, 96, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1), groups=96, bias=False)
              (act): GELU(approximate='none')
              (fc2): Linear(in_features=96, out_features=96, bias=True)
            )
          )
        )
        (norm): LayerNorm((96,), eps=1e-05, elementwise_affine=True)
      )
    )
    (c): Sequential(
      (0): Conv2d(96, 96, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
      (1): LeakyReLU(negative_slope=0.05, inplace=True)
    )
    (tesa_out): TESA(
      (esa1): ESA(
        (conv1): Conv2d(96, 24, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
        (conv2): Conv2d(24, 24, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(2, 2))
        (conv3): Conv2d(24, 24, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (conv4): Conv2d(24, 96, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (sigmoid): Sigmoid()
        (relu): ReLU(inplace=True)
      )
      (esa2): ESA(
        (conv1): Conv2d(96, 24, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
        (conv2): Conv2d(24, 24, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(2, 2))
        (conv3): Conv2d(24, 24, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (conv4): Conv2d(24, 96, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (sigmoid): Sigmoid()
        (relu): ReLU(inplace=True)
      )
      (esa3): ESA(
        (conv1): Conv2d(96, 24, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
        (conv2): Conv2d(24, 24, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(2, 2))
        (conv3): Conv2d(24, 24, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (conv4): Conv2d(24, 96, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (sigmoid): Sigmoid()
        (relu): ReLU(inplace=True)
      )
    )
  )
  (LR_conv1): Conv2d(96, 96, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
  (LR_conv2): Conv2d(96, 96, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
  (pixel_shuffle): PixelShuffle(upscale_factor=4)
  (recon_conv1): Conv2d(96, 96, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
  (recon_conv2): Conv2d(96, 48, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
)
